getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            cmd = f\"SELECT * FROM {table_name}\"\n            if filter_conditions:\n                where = \" AND \".join(f\"{k}='{self._sql_escape(v)}'\" for k, v in filter_conditions.items())\n                cmd += f\" WHERE {where}\"\n            if order_by:\n                cmd += f\" ORDER BY {order_by} {'DESC' if desc else 'ASC'}\"\n            if limit:\n                cmd += f\" LIMIT {limit}\"\n            result = self.db.query(cmd)\n            return result.get('rows', [])\n        else:\n            # SQLAlchemy ORM\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            if filter_conditions:\n                for key, value in filter_conditions.items():\n                    query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            if order_by:\n                order_col = getattr(model_class, order_by)\n                if desc:\n                    order_col = order_col.desc()\n                query = query.order_by(order_col)\n            if limit:\n                query = query.limit(limit)\n            return query.all()\n\n    def _get_model_by_id(self, model_class, record_id: str, \n                        id_field='id', extra_filters=None) -> any:\n        \"\"\"Get single record by ID, works with both SQLAlchemy and KosDB.\"\"\"\n        if self.db is None:\n            return None\n        \n        filters = {id_field: record_id}\n        if extra_filters:\n            filters.update(extra_filters)\n        \n        # Extra defensive check: if self.db is a dict, always use KosDB path.\n        is_kosdb = self._is_kosdb() or isinstance(self.db, dict)\n        \n        if is_kosdb:\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            where = \" AND \".join(f\"{k}='{self._sql_escape(v)}'\" for k, v in filters.items())\n            cmd = f\"SELECT * FROM {table_name} WHERE {where}\"\n            result = self.db.query(cmd)\n            rows = result.get('rows', [])\n            return rows[0] if rows else None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n        if isinstance(page, dict):\n            author = page.get('author')\n            if isinstance(author, dict):\n                author = author.get('display_name')\n            return {\n                \"id\": page.get('id'),\n                \"title\": page.get('title'),\n                \"slug\": page.get('slug'),\n                \"status\": page.get('status'),\n                \"author\": author,\n                \"updated_at\": page.get('updated_at')\n            }\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n        if isinstance(post, dict):\n            author = post.get('author')\n            if isinstance(author, dict):\n                author = author.get('display_name')\n            return {\n                \"id\": post.get('id'),\n                \"title\": post.get('title'),\n                \"slug\": post.get('slug'),\n                \"status\": post.get('status'),\n                \"author\": author,\n                \"updated_at\": post.get('updated_at')\n            }\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.update_page(page_id, **data)\n            if not page:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_page(page_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    def list_posts(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Post\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"posts\": []})\n        posts = self._get_model_list(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"posts\": [self._serialize_post(p) for p in posts]})\n\n    def create_post(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.create_post(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.update_post(post_id, **data)\n            if not post:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_post(post_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    # ---------------- Media ----------------\n\n    def list_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"media\": []})\n        media = self._get_model_list(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for m in media:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(m, dict):\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"filename\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"url\": m.get('file_url'),\n                    \"mime_type\": m.get('mime_type'),\n                    \"width\": m.get('width'),\n                    \"height\": m.get('height')\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.id,\n                    \"name\": m.filename,\n                    \"filename\": m.filename,\n                    \"url\": m.file_url,\n                    \"mime_type\": m.mime_type,\n                    \"width\": m.width,\n                    \"height\": m.height\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"media\": result})\n\n    def upload_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"uploaded\": True}, 201)\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            # request.files may contain multipart uploads\n            files = getattr(request, \"files\", {}) or {}\n            uploaded = []\n            for name, file_storage in files.items():\n                media = manager.upload(file_storage, uploaded_by=None)\n                uploaded.append({\"id\": media.id, \"name\": media.filename, \"mime_type\": media.mime_type})\n            return Response.json({\"uploaded\": uploaded}, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_media(self, request: Request, media_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            if manager.delete(media_id):\n                return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n            return Response.not_found()\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 500)\n\n    # ---------------- Plugins ----------------\n\n    def list_plugins(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        try:\n            registry = get_registry()\n            plugins = registry.list_available(installed_only=False)\n            result = []\n            for p in plugins:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": p.name,\n                    \"name\": p.name,\n                    \"version\": p.version,\n                    \"description\": p.description,\n                    \"active\": p.active,\n                    \"installed\": p.installed\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": []})\n\n    def activate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.activate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def deactivate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.deactivate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": not success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def delete_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.uninstall(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Templates & Themes ----------------\n\n    def list_templates(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.engine import TemplateEngine\n        try:\n            engine = TemplateEngine()\n            templates = engine.list_templates()\n            result = []\n            for t in templates:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"path\": t.get(\"path\", \"\"),\n                    \"updated_at\": t.get(\"updated_at\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": []})\n\n    def create_template(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True, \"data\": data}, 201)\n\n    def update_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"updated\": True, \"data\": data})\n\n    def delete_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"deleted\": True})\n\n    def list_themes(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            themes = tm.list_themes()\n            result = []\n            for t in themes:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"description\": t.get(\"description\", \"\"),\n                    \"active\": t.get(\"active\", False)\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": []})\n\n    def activate_theme(self, request: Request, theme_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            tm.activate(theme_id)\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": False, \"error\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Users & Roles ----------------\n\n    def list_users(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"users\": []})\n        users = self._get_model_list(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for u in users:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(u, dict):\n                roles = u.get('roles', [])\n                role_name = roles[0].get('name') if roles and isinstance(roles[0], dict) else (\n                    roles[0].name if roles else \"user\"\n                )\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": u.get('id'),\n                    \"username\": u.get('username'),\n                    \"email\": u.get('email'),\n                    \"display_name\": u.get('display_name'),\n                    \"role\": role_name,\n                    \"is_active\": u.get('is_active'),\n                    \"roles\": [r.get('name') if isinstance(r, dict) else r.name for r in roles]\n                })\n            else:\n                role_name = u.roles[0].name if u.roles else \"user\"\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": u.id,\n                    \"username\": u.username,\n                    \"email\": u.email,\n                    \"display_name\": u.display_name,\n                    \"role\": role_name,\n                    \"is_active\": u.is_active,\n                    \"roles\": [r.name for r in u.roles]\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"users\": result})\n\n    def create_user(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n\n        try:\n            hasher = PasswordHasher()\n            user = User(\n                username=data.get(\"username\"),\n                email=data.get(\"email\"),\n                password_hash=hasher.hash(data.get(\"password\", \"changeme\")),\n                display_name=data.get(\"display_name\", data.get(\"username\")),\n                is_active=data.get(\"is_active\", True)\n            )\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: use SQL INSERT\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                cols = ['id', 'username', 'email', 'password_hash', 'display_name', 'is_active', 'is_deleted']\n                user.id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n                vals = [\n                    user.id, user.username, user.email, user.password_hash,\n                    user.display_name, 1 if user.is_active else 0, 0\n                ]\n                val_str = \", \".join(f\"'{self._sql_escape(v)}'\" for v in vals)\n                cmd = f\"INSERT INTO {table_name} ({', '.join(cols)}) VALUES ({val_str})\"\n                self.db.execute(cmd)\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                self.db.add(user)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\n                \"id\": user.id if hasattr(user, 'id') else user.get('id'),\n                \"username\": user.username if hasattr(user, 'username') else user.get('username'),\n                \"email\": user.email if hasattr(user, 'email') else user.get('email'),\n                \"role\": \"user\",\n                \"is_active\": user.is_active if hasattr(user, 'is_active') else user.get('is_active')\n            }, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"updated\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: build UPDATE\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                sets = []\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    if key == \"is_active\":\n                        value = 1 if value else 0\n                    elif key == \"password\" and value:\n                        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n                        key = \"password_hash\"\n                        value = PasswordHasher().hash(value)\n                    sets.append(f\"{key}='{self._sql_escape(value)}'\")\n                if sets:\n                    cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(user_id)}' AND is_deleted='0'\"\n                    self.db.execute(cmd)\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": user_id,\n                    \"username\": data.get(\"username\", user.get('username') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.username),\n                    \"email\": data.get(\"email\", user.get('email') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.email),\n                    \"role\": \"user\",\n                    \"is_active\": data.get(\"is_active\", user.get('is_active') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.is_active)\n                })\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                if \"username\" in data:\n                    user.username = data[\"username\"]\n                if \"email\" in data:\n                    user.email = data[\"email\"]\n                if \"display_name\" in data:\n                    user.display_name = data[\"display_name\"]\n                if \"is_active\" in data:\n                    user.is_active = bool(data[\"is_active\"])\n                if \"password\" in data and data[\"password\"]:\n                    from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n                    user.password_hash = PasswordHasher().hash(data[\"password\"])\n                self.db.commit()\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": user.id,\n                    \"username\": user.username,\n                    \"email\": user.email,\n                    \"role\": user.roles[0].name if user.roles else \"user\",\n                    \"is_active\": user.is_active\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET is_deleted='1' WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(user_id)}'\"\n                self.db.execute(cmd)\n            else:\n                user.is_deleted = True\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def list_roles(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"roles\": []})\n        roles = self._get_model_list(Role, order_by=\"name\")\n        result = []\n        for r in roles:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(r, dict):\n                perms = r.get('permissions', '')\n                perms_list = [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": r.get('name'),\n                    \"description\": r.get('description'),\n                    \"permissions\": perms_list\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.id,\n                    \"name\": r.name,\n                    \"description\": r.description,\n                    \"permissions\": r.permissions_list\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"roles\": result})\n\n    def update_role(self, request: Request, role_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": role_id, \"updated\": True})\n        role = self._get_model_by_id(Role, role_id)\n        if not role:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: build UPDATE\n                table_name = getattr(Role, '__tablename__', 'roles')\n                sets = []\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    sets.append(f\"permissions='{self._sql_escape(','.join(data['permissions']))}'\")\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    sets.append(f\"description='{self._sql_escape(data['description'])}'\")\n                if sets:\n                    cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(role_id)}'\"\n                    self.db.execute(cmd)\n                perms = ','.join(data.get('permissions', []))\n                if isinstance(role, dict):\n                    return Response.json({\n                        \"id\": role.get('id'),\n                        \"name\": role.get('name'),\n                        \"description\": data.get('description', role.get('description')),\n                        \"permissions\": [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                    })\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.id,\n                    \"name\": role.name,\n                    \"description\": data.get('description', role.description),\n                    \"permissions\": [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                })\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    role.permissions = \",\".join(data[\"permissions\"])\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    role.description = data[\"description\"]\n                self.db.commit()\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.id,\n                    \"name\": role.name,\n                    \"description\": role.description,\n                    \"permissions\": role.permissions_list\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Settings ----------------\n\n    def get_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        defaults = {\n            \"site_name\": \"WebCMS\",\n            \"site_url\": \"https://example.com\",\n            \"admin_email\": \"admin@example.com\",\n            \"default_language\": \"en\",\n            \"posts_per_page\": 10,\n            \"cache_enabled\": True,\n            \"cache_ttl\": 300,\n            \"search_enabled\": True,\n            \"elasticsearch_url\": \"http://localhost:9200\",\n            \"notifications_enabled\": True,\n            \"smtp_host\": \"localhost\",\n            \"smtp_port\": 587,\n            \"smtp_user\": \"\",\n            \"smtp_pass\": \"\",\n            \"csp_enabled\": True,\n            \"require_https\": False\n        }\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                result = self.db.query(\"SELECT * FROM settings\")\n                settings = result.get('rows', [])\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.get('key')] = self._coerce_setting(s.get('value'), s.get('type'))\n            else:\n                settings = self.db.query(Setting).all()\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.key] = self._coerce_setting(s.value, s.type)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n\n    def update_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    type_ = self._guess_type(value)\n                    val_str = self._sql_escape(str(value))\n                    # Try UPDATE first, then INSERT if no rows affected\n                    update_cmd = f\"UPDATE settings SET value='{val_str}', type='{type_}' WHERE key='{self._sql_escape(key)}'\"\n                    update_result = self.db.execute(update_cmd)\n                    # KosDB returns something like \"OK 1 row(s) affected\" or \"OK 0 row(s) affected\"\n                    if \"0 row\" in str(update_result) or \"Empty set\" in str(update_result):\n                        insert_cmd = f\"INSERT INTO settings (key, value, type) VALUES ('{self._sql_escape(key)}', '{val_str}', '{type_}')\"\n                        self.db.execute(insert_cmd)\n            else:\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    existing = self.db.query(Setting).filter(Setting.key == key).first()\n                    if existing:\n                        existing.value = str(value)\n                        existing.type = self._guess_type(value)\n                    else:\n                        setting = Setting(key=key, value=str(value), type=self._guess_type(value))\n                        self.db.add(setting)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _guess_type(value):\n        if isinstance(value, bool):\n            return \"bool\"\n        if isinstance(value, int):\n            return \"int\"\n        if isinstance(value, float):\n            return \"float\"\n        return \"str\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _coerce_setting(value, type_):\n        if type_ == \"bool\":\n            return str(value).lower() in (\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\", \"on\")\n        if type_ == \"int\":\n            try:\n                return int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                return 0\n        if type_ == \"float\":\n            try:\n                return float(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                return 0.0\n        return value\n\n    # ---------------- Cache ----------------\n\n    def cache_stats(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.cache.manager import get_tenant_cache\n        try:\n            cache = get_tenant_cache(\"default\")\n            stats = cache.get_stats() if hasattr(cache, \"get_stats\") else {}\n            return Response.json({\n                \"keys\": stats.get(\"keys\", 0),\n                \"hit_rate\": stats.get(\"hit_rate\", 0),\n                \"memory\": stats.get(\"memory\", \"0B\"),\n                \"evicted\": stats.get(\"evicted\", 0)\n            })\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"keys\": 0, \"hit_rate\": 0, \"memory\": \"0B\", \"evicted\": 0})\n\n    def cache_warm(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"warmed\": 0})\n\n    def cache_invalidate(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.cache.manager import get_tenant_cache\n        data = request.json or {}\n        pattern = data.get(\"pattern\", \"*\")\n        try:\n            cache = get_tenant_cache(\"default\")\n            if hasattr(cache, \"invalidate_pattern\"):\n                deleted = cache.invalidate_pattern(pattern)\n            else:\n                cache.clear()\n                deleted = 0\n            return Response.json({\"deleted\": deleted, \"pattern\": pattern})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"deleted\": 0, \"pattern\": pattern})\n\n    # ---------------- Backups ----------------\n\n    def list_backups(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n            engine = BackupEngine(self.db)\n            backups = engine.list_backups()\n            return Response.json({\"backups\": backups})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"backups\": []})\n\n    def create_backup(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n            engine = BackupEngine(self.db)\n            backup_id = engine.create_backup()\n            return Response.json({\"id\": backup_id, \"created\": True}, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n\n    def restore_backup(self, request: Request, backup_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n            engine = BackupEngine(self.db)\n            engine.restore_backup(backup_id)\n \"restored\": False, \"message\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    def verify_backup(self, request: Request, backup_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n    return Response.json({\"id\": backup_id, \"valid\": False})\n\n    def delete_backup(self, request: Request, backup_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.workflow.manager import WorkflowManager as WM\n        try:\n            manager = WM(self.db)\n            instances = manager.list_instances()\n inst.get(\"content_title\", \"Untitled\"),\n                    \"state\": inst.get(\"state\", \"draft\"),\n                    \"reviewer\": inst.get(\"reviewer\"),\n definitions = manager.list_definitions()\n            result = []\n            for d in definitions:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": d.get(\"id\"),\n Response.json({\"definitions\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"definitions\": []})\n\n    def workflow_transition(self, request: Request, instance_id: str) -> manager.transition(instance_id, data.get(\"action\"))\n            return Response.json({\"id\": instance_id, \"message\": f\"Transitioned to {data.get('action')}\"})\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": instance_id, \"message\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    def workflow_assign(self, request: Request, instance_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.workflow.manager import WorkflowManager as WM\n        data = request.json or {}\n        try:\n            manager = WM(self.db)\n            manager.assign(instance_id, data.get(\"reviewer_id\"))\n            return isinstance(self.db, dict)\n        \n        if is_kosdb:\n            table_name = getattr(model_class, '__tablename__', \n                                getattr(model_class, '__name__', '').lower() + 's')\n            where = \" AND \".join(\n                self._kosdb_where_clause(k, v) for k, v in filters.items()\n            )\n            cmd = f\"SELECT * FROM {table_name} WHERE {where}\"\n            result = self.db.query(cmd)\n            if result.get('error'):\n                return None\n            rows = result.get('rows', [])\n            return rows[0] if rows else None\n        else:\n            query = self.db.query(model_class)\n            for key, value in filters.items():\n                query = query.filter(getattr(model_class, key) == value)\n            return query.first()\n\n    # ---------------- Dashboard ----------------\n\n    def dashboard(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        \"\"\"Return dashboard widgets with real-ish stats.\"\"\"\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.models.content import Post, Page\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n\n        stats = {}\n        if self.db:\n            stats = {\n                \"users\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"active\": self._get_model_count(User, {\"is_deleted\": False, \"is_active\": True})\n                },\n                \"content\": {\n                    \"posts\": self._get_model_count(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}),\n                    \"pages\": self._get_model_count(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                },\n                \"media\": {\n                    \"total\": self._get_model_count(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False})\n                }\n            }\n\n        registry = get_widget_registry()\n        configs = [\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"stats\", title=\"Content Statistics\", type=\"stats\", position=\"main\"),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"activity\", title=\"Recent Activity\", type=\"activity\", position=\"main\", refresh_interval=60),\n            WidgetConfig(id=\"health\", title=\"System Health\", type=\"health\", position=\"sidebar\", refresh_interval=30),\n        ]\n        widgets = registry.render_all(self.db, configs) if self.db else [\n            {\"id\": \"stats\", \"title\": \"Content Statistics\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcca\", \"data\": stats},\n            {\"id\": \"activity\", \"title\": \"Recent Activity\", \"icon\": \"\ud83d\udcc5\", \"data\": {\"recent_posts\": 0}},\n            {\"id\": \"health\", \"title\": \"System Health\", \"icon\": \"\u2764\ufe0f\", \"data\": {\"status\": \"ok\"}}\n        ]\n\n        # Ensure every widget has the keys Dashboard.jsx expects\n        for i, widget in enumerate(widgets):\n            widget.setdefault(\"icon\", \"\")\n            widget.setdefault(\"data\", \"\")\n\n        return Response.json({\"widgets\": widgets})\n\n    # ---------------- Content: Pages & Posts ----------------\n\n    def _serialize_page(self, page) -> dict:\n        # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n        if isinstance(page, dict):\n            author = page.get('author')\n            if isinstance(author, dict):\n                author = author.get('display_name')\n            return {\n                \"id\": page.get('id'),\n                \"title\": page.get('title'),\n                \"slug\": page.get('slug'),\n                \"status\": page.get('status'),\n                \"author\": author,\n                \"updated_at\": page.get('updated_at')\n            }\n        return {\n            \"id\": page.id,\n            \"title\": page.title,\n            \"slug\": page.slug,\n            \"status\": page.status,\n            \"author\": page.author.display_name if page.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": page.updated_at.isoformat() if page.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def _serialize_post(self, post) -> dict:\n        # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n        if isinstance(post, dict):\n            author = post.get('author')\n            if isinstance(author, dict):\n                author = author.get('display_name')\n            return {\n                \"id\": post.get('id'),\n                \"title\": post.get('title'),\n                \"slug\": post.get('slug'),\n                \"status\": post.get('status'),\n                \"author\": author,\n                \"updated_at\": post.get('updated_at')\n            }\n        return {\n            \"id\": post.id,\n            \"title\": post.title,\n            \"slug\": post.slug,\n            \"status\": post.status,\n            \"author\": post.author.display_name if post.author else None,\n            \"updated_at\": post.updated_at.isoformat() if post.updated_at else None\n        }\n\n    def list_pages(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Page\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"pages\": []})\n        pages = self._get_model_list(Page, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"pages\": [self._serialize_page(p) for p in pages]})\n\n    def create_page(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.create_page(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            page = manager.update_page(page_id, **data)\n            if not page:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_page(page))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_page(self, request: Request, page_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_page(page_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": page_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    def list_posts(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.content import Post\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"posts\": []})\n        posts = self._get_model_list(Post, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"updated_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        return Response.json({\"posts\": [self._serialize_post(p) for p in posts]})\n\n    def create_post(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.create_post(**data)\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post), 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"updated\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            post = manager.update_post(post_id, **data)\n            if not post:\n                return Response.not_found()\n            return Response.json(self._serialize_post(post))\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_post(self, request: Request, post_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.content.manager import ContentManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = ContentManager(self.db)\n        if manager.delete_post(post_id):\n            return Response.json({\"id\": post_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        return Response.not_found()\n\n    # ---------------- Media ----------------\n\n    def list_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.media import Media\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"media\": []})\n        media = self._get_model_list(Media, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for m in media:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(m, dict):\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"filename\": m.get('filename'),\n                    \"url\": m.get('file_url'),\n                    \"mime_type\": m.get('mime_type'),\n                    \"width\": m.get('width'),\n                    \"height\": m.get('height')\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": m.id,\n                    \"name\": m.filename,\n                    \"filename\": m.filename,\n                    \"url\": m.file_url,\n                    \"mime_type\": m.mime_type,\n                    \"width\": m.width,\n                    \"height\": m.height\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"media\": result})\n\n    def upload_media(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"uploaded\": True}, 201)\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            # request.files may contain multipart uploads\n            files = getattr(request, \"files\", {}) or {}\n            uploaded = []\n            for name, file_storage in files.items():\n                media = manager.upload(file_storage, uploaded_by=None)\n                uploaded.append({\"id\": media.id, \"name\": media.filename, \"mime_type\": media.mime_type})\n            return Response.json({\"uploaded\": uploaded}, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_media(self, request: Request, media_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.media.manager import MediaManager\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        manager = MediaManager(self.db)\n        try:\n            if manager.delete(media_id):\n                return Response.json({\"id\": media_id, \"deleted\": True})\n            return Response.not_found()\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.error(str(e), 500)\n\n    # ---------------- Plugins ----------------\n\n    def list_plugins(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        try:\n            registry = get_registry()\n            plugins = registry.list_available(installed_only=False)\n            result = []\n            for p in plugins:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": p.name,\n                    \"name\": p.name,\n                    \"version\": p.version,\n                    \"description\": p.description,\n                    \"active\": p.active,\n                    \"installed\": p.installed\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"plugins\": []})\n\n    def activate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.activate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def deactivate_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.deactivate(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id, \"active\": not success}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    def delete_plugin(self, request: Request, plugin_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.plugins.marketplace import get_registry\n        registry = get_registry()\n        success, message = registry.uninstall(plugin_id)\n        return Response.json({\"success\": success, \"message\": message, \"id\": plugin_id}, 200 if success else 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Templates & Themes ----------------\n\n    def list_templates(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.engine import TemplateEngine\n        try:\n            engine = TemplateEngine()\n            templates = engine.list_templates()\n            result = []\n            for t in templates:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"path\": t.get(\"path\", \"\"),\n                    \"updated_at\": t.get(\"updated_at\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"templates\": []})\n\n    def create_template(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True, \"data\": data}, 201)\n\n    def update_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        data = request.json or {}\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"updated\": True, \"data\": data})\n\n    def delete_template(self, request: Request, template_id: str) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"id\": template_id, \"deleted\": True})\n\n    def list_themes(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            themes = tm.list_themes()\n            result = []\n            for t in themes:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": t.get(\"id\", t.get(\"name\")),\n                    \"name\": t.get(\"name\"),\n                    \"description\": t.get(\"description\", \"\"),\n                    \"active\": t.get(\"active\", False)\n                })\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": result})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"themes\": []})\n\n    def activate_theme(self, request: Request, theme_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.templates.theme import ThemeManager as TM\n        try:\n            tm = TM()\n            tm.activate(theme_id)\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": theme_id, \"active\": False, \"error\": str(e)}, 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Users & Roles ----------------\n\n    def list_users(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"users\": []})\n        users = self._get_model_list(User, {\"is_deleted\": False}, order_by=\"created_at\", limit=50, desc=True)\n        result = []\n        for u in users:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(u, dict):\n                roles = u.get('roles', [])\n                role_name = roles[0].get('name') if roles and isinstance(roles[0], dict) else (\n                    roles[0].name if roles else \"user\"\n                )\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": u.get('id'),\n                    \"username\": u.get('username'),\n                    \"email\": u.get('email'),\n                    \"display_name\": u.get('display_name'),\n                    \"role\": role_name,\n                    \"is_active\": u.get('is_active'),\n                    \"roles\": [r.get('name') if isinstance(r, dict) else r.name for r in roles]\n                })\n            else:\n                role_name = u.roles[0].name if u.roles else \"user\"\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": u.id,\n                    \"username\": u.username,\n                    \"email\": u.email,\n                    \"display_name\": u.display_name,\n                    \"role\": role_name,\n                    \"is_active\": u.is_active,\n                    \"roles\": [r.name for r in u.roles]\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"users\": result})\n\n    def create_user(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not data:\n            return Response.error(\"Invalid JSON\", 400)\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": str(uuid.uuid4()), \"created\": True}, 201)\n\n        try:\n            hasher = PasswordHasher()\n            user = User(\n                username=data.get(\"username\"),\n                email=data.get(\"email\"),\n                password_hash=hasher.hash(data.get(\"password\", \"changeme\")),\n                display_name=data.get(\"display_name\", data.get(\"username\")),\n                is_active=data.get(\"is_active\", True)\n            )\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: use SQL INSERT\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                cols = ['id', 'username', 'email', 'password_hash', 'display_name', 'is_active', 'is_deleted']\n                user.id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n                vals = [\n                    user.id, user.username, user.email, user.password_hash,\n                    user.display_name, 1 if user.is_active else 0, 0\n                ]\n                val_str = \", \".join(f\"'{self._sql_escape(v)}'\" for v in vals)\n                cmd = f\"INSERT INTO {table_name} ({', '.join(cols)}) VALUES ({val_str})\"\n                self.db.execute(cmd)\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                self.db.add(user)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\n                \"id\": user.id if hasattr(user, 'id') else user.get('id'),\n                \"username\": user.username if hasattr(user, 'username') else user.get('username'),\n                \"email\": user.email if hasattr(user, 'email') else user.get('email'),\n                \"role\": \"user\",\n                \"is_active\": user.is_active if hasattr(user, 'is_active') else user.get('is_active')\n            }, 201)\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def update_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"updated\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: build UPDATE\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                sets = []\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    if key == \"is_active\":\n                        value = 1 if value else 0\n                    elif key == \"password\" and value:\n                        from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n                        key = \"password_hash\"\n                        value = PasswordHasher().hash(value)\n                    sets.append(f\"{key}='{self._sql_escape(value)}'\")\n                if sets:\n                    cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(user_id)}' AND is_deleted='0'\"\n                    self.db.execute(cmd)\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": user_id,\n                    \"username\": data.get(\"username\", user.get('username') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.username),\n                    \"email\": data.get(\"email\", user.get('email') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.email),\n                    \"role\": \"user\",\n                    \"is_active\": data.get(\"is_active\", user.get('is_active') if isinstance(user, dict) else user.is_active)\n                })\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                if \"username\" in data:\n                    user.username = data[\"username\"]\n                if \"email\" in data:\n                    user.email = data[\"email\"]\n                if \"display_name\" in data:\n                    user.display_name = data[\"display_name\"]\n                if \"is_active\" in data:\n                    user.is_active = bool(data[\"is_active\"])\n                if \"password\" in data and data[\"password\"]:\n                    from webcms.auth.password import PasswordHasher\n                    user.password_hash = PasswordHasher().hash(data[\"password\"])\n                self.db.commit()\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": user.id,\n                    \"username\": user.username,\n                    \"email\": user.email,\n                    \"role\": user.roles[0].name if user.roles else \"user\",\n                    \"is_active\": user.is_active\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def delete_user(self, request: Request, user_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import User\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        user = self._get_model_by_id(User, user_id, extra_filters={\"is_deleted\": False})\n        if not user:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                table_name = getattr(User, '__tablename__', 'users')\n                cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET is_deleted='1' WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(user_id)}'\"\n                self.db.execute(cmd)\n            else:\n                user.is_deleted = True\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\"id\": user_id, \"deleted\": True})\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    def list_roles(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"roles\": []})\n        roles = self._get_model_list(Role, order_by=\"name\")\n        result = []\n        for r in roles:\n            # Handle both dict (KosDB) and object (SQLAlchemy) access\n            if isinstance(r, dict):\n                perms = r.get('permissions', '')\n                perms_list = [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.get('id'),\n                    \"name\": r.get('name'),\n                    \"description\": r.get('description'),\n                    \"permissions\": perms_list\n                })\n            else:\n                result.append({\n                    \"id\": r.id,\n                    \"name\": r.name,\n                    \"description\": r.description,\n                    \"permissions\": r.permissions_list\n                })\n        return Response.json({\"roles\": result})\n\n    def update_role(self, request: Request, role_id: str) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.user import Role\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"id\": role_id, \"updated\": True})\n        role = self._get_model_by_id(Role, role_id)\n        if not role:\n            return Response.not_found()\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                # KosDB: build UPDATE\n                table_name = getattr(Role, '__tablename__', 'roles')\n                sets = []\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    sets.append(f\"permissions='{self._sql_escape(','.join(data['permissions']))}'\")\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    sets.append(f\"description='{self._sql_escape(data['description'])}'\")\n                if sets:\n                    cmd = f\"UPDATE {table_name} SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id='{self._sql_escape(role_id)}'\"\n                    self.db.execute(cmd)\n                perms = ','.join(data.get('permissions', []))\n                if isinstance(role, dict):\n                    return Response.json({\n                        \"id\": role.get('id'),\n                        \"name\": role.get('name'),\n                        \"description\": data.get('description', role.get('description')),\n                        \"permissions\": [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                    })\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.id,\n                    \"name\": role.name,\n                    \"description\": data.get('description', role.description),\n                    \"permissions\": [p.strip() for p in perms.split(',')] if perms else []\n                })\n            else:\n                # SQLAlchemy\n                if \"permissions\" in data:\n                    role.permissions = \",\".join(data[\"permissions\"])\n                if \"description\" in data:\n                    role.description = data[\"description\"]\n                self.db.commit()\n                return Response.json({\n                    \"id\": role.id,\n                    \"name\": role.name,\n                    \"description\": role.description,\n                    \"permissions\": role.permissions_list\n                })\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    # ---------------- Settings ----------------\n\n    def get_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        defaults = {\n            \"site_name\": \"WebCMS\",\n            \"site_url\": \"https://example.com\",\n            \"admin_email\": \"admin@example.com\",\n            \"default_language\": \"en\",\n            \"posts_per_page\": 10,\n            \"cache_enabled\": True,\n            \"cache_ttl\": 300,\n            \"search_enabled\": True,\n            \"elasticsearch_url\": \"http://localhost:9200\",\n            \"notifications_enabled\": True,\n            \"smtp_host\": \"localhost\",\n            \"smtp_port\": 587,\n            \"smtp_user\": \"\",\n            \"smtp_pass\": \"\",\n            \"csp_enabled\": True,\n            \"require_https\": False\n        }\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                result = self.db.query(\"SELECT * FROM settings\")\n                settings = result.get('rows', [])\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.get('key')] = self._coerce_setting(s.get('value'), s.get('type'))\n            else:\n                settings = self.db.query(Setting).all()\n                for s in settings:\n                    defaults[s.key] = self._coerce_setting(s.value, s.type)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return Response.json({\"settings\": defaults})\n\n    def update_settings(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.models.system import Setting\n        data = request.json or {}\n        if not self.db:\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        try:\n            if self._is_kosdb():\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    type_ = self._guess_type(value)\n                    val_str = self._sql_escape(str(value))\n                    # Try UPDATE first, then INSERT if no rows affected\n                    update_cmd = f\"UPDATE settings SET value='{val_str}', type='{type_}' WHERE key='{self._sql_escape(key)}'\"\n                    update_result = self.db.execute(update_cmd)\n                    # KosDB returns something like \"OK 1 row(s) affected\" or \"OK 0 row(s) affected\"\n                    if \"0 row\" in str(update_result) or \"Empty set\" in str(update_result):\n                        insert_cmd = f\"INSERT INTO settings (key, value, type) VALUES ('{self._sql_escape(key)}', '{val_str}', '{type_}')\"\n                        self.db.execute(insert_cmd)\n            else:\n                for key, value in data.items():\n                    existing = self.db.query(Setting).filter(Setting.key == key).first()\n                    if existing:\n                        existing.value = str(value)\n                        existing.type = self._guess_type(value)\n                    else:\n                        setting = Setting(key=key, value=str(value), type=self._guess_type(value))\n                        self.db.add(setting)\n                self.db.commit()\n            return Response.json({\"updated\": True, \"settings\": data})\n        except Exception as e:\n            if not self._is_kosdb():\n                self.db.rollback()\n            return Response.error(str(e), 400)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _guess_type(value):\n        if isinstance(value, bool):\n            return \"bool\"\n        if isinstance(value, int):\n            return \"int\"\n        if isinstance(value, float):\n            return \"float\"\n        return \"str\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _coerce_setting(value, type_):\n        if type_ == \"bool\":\n            return str(value).lower() in (\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\", \"on\")\n        if type_ == \"int\":\n            try:\n                return int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                return 0\n        if type_ == \"float\":\n            try:\n                return float(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                return 0.0\n        return value\n\n    # ---------------- Cache ----------------\n\n    def cache_stats(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.cache.manager import get_tenant_cache\n        try:\n            cache = get_tenant_cache(\"default\")\n            stats = cache.get_stats() if hasattr(cache, \"get_stats\") else {}\n            return Response.json({\n                \"keys\": stats.get(\"keys\", 0),\n                \"hit_rate\": stats.get(\"hit_rate\", 0),\n                \"memory\": stats.get(\"memory\", \"0B\"),\n                \"evicted\": stats.get(\"evicted\", 0)\n            })\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"keys\": 0, \"hit_rate\": 0, \"memory\": \"0B\", \"evicted\": 0})\n\n    def cache_warm(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        return Response.json({\"warmed\": 0})\n\n    def cache_invalidate(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.cache.manager import get_tenant_cache\n        data = request.json or {}\n        pattern = data.get(\"pattern\", \"*\")\n        try:\n            cache = get_tenant_cache(\"default\")\n            if hasattr(cache, \"invalidate_pattern\"):\n                deleted = cache.invalidate_pattern(pattern)\n            else:\n                cache.clear()\n                deleted = 0\n            return Response.json({\"deleted\": deleted, \"pattern\": pattern})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"deleted\": 0, \"pattern\": pattern})\n\n    # ---------------- Backups ----------------\n\n    def list_backups(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n            engine = BackupEngine(self.db)\n            backups = engine.list_backups()\n            return Response.json({\"backups\": backups})\n        except Exception:\n            return Response.json({\"backups\": []})\n\n    def create_backup(self, request: Request) -> Response:\n        from webcms.backup.engine import BackupEngine\n        try:\n            engine = BackupEngine(self.db)\n            backup_id = engine.create_backup()\n            return Response.json({\"id\": backup_id,
